We need implement gradients carefully.

Function `gradient_matrix(h, n)`:
length m=2n-1. G = np.zeros((n,m))
For l from -(n-1) to n-1, idx=n-1+l:
 if l>=0:
   # term p first: p+l valid for p=0..n-1-l -> G[:n-l, idx] += 1 - h[l:]
   # term p second: p-l valid for p=l..n-1 -> G[:,?]? For p=l..n-1, row p, term h_{p-l}(1-h_p) -> derivative -h_{p-l}
   G[:n-l, idx] += 1 - h[l:]
   G[l:, idx] -= h[:-l]
 else:
   l negative, set s=-l.
   # first: p+s valid p=0..n-1-s -> 1-h[s:]
   G[:n-s, idx] += 1 - h[s:]
   # second: p-s valid p=s..n-1 -> -h[:n-s]
   G[s:, idx] -= h[:n-s]
This can be done loop over l. For l=0, the two add both to same rows, giving (1-h)-h=1-2h, correct. But first slice G[:n,0] +=1-h, second G[:,0]-=h -> same.
We can perhaps compute G via correlation of ones? But loop fine.

For LP constraints: b = G @ h - c. c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full') (raw). b = G.dot(h) - c. Check: G @ h = ∑_p G_l,p h_p = sum derivative terms linear approximations. At u=h, affine = c_l - G·h + G·h = c_l. Good.
A_ub rows: [G_l, -1] variable x=[u,t]: G_l·u - t ≤ G_l·h - c_l. In scipy, A_ub shape (2n-1, n+1). A_ub[:, :n]=G, A_ub[:,n]=-1. b_ub=Gh - c.
Objective c_obj = zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.
Bounds: [(0,1)]*n + [(None,None)] maybe. For linprog bounds list of tuples; None allowed. Equality A_eq = np.hstack([ones(1,n), [0]]), b_eq=[n/2].
Method='highs'. options maybe presolve=True.

Let's specify coordinate descent best.

`cd_opt(h, n, max_passes, deadline, gs_iters=35)`:
- h copy feasible.
- best_c = raw max c * dx.
- For pass in range(max_passes): if time > deadline break.

Pseudo:
```
def gradient_matrix(h,n):
 M=2*n-1
 G=np.empty((n,M))
 for m,s in enumerate(range(n-1,-n,-1)?): # s = n-1-m
   G[:,m]=0
   if s>=0:
      G[:n-s, m] += 1 - h[s:]
      G[s:, m] -= h[:n-s]
   else:
      ss=-s
      G[ss:, m] += 1 - h[:n-ss]
      G[:n-ss, m] -= h[ss:]
```
Check for s positive: p+s valid p=0..n-s-1 => G[:n-s] +=1-h[s:]; p-s valid p=s..n-1 => G[s:] -= h[:n-s]. Actually if s=-ss, p-s=p+ss valid p=0..n-1-ss => G[:n-ss] -= h[ss:]. Need re-evaluate formula:
For s>=0: q1=p+s => valid p < n-s -> h[s:]; q2=p-s => valid p>=s -> h[:n-s].
For s<0, ss=-s: q1=p-ss valid p>=ss -> h[p-ss] with p=ss..n-1 => rows ss:, h[:n-ss]? For p=ss, index0; p=n-1, index n-1-ss -> length n-ss. So G[ss:] += 1-h[:n-ss]. q2=p+ss valid p < n-ss -> rows :n-ss, h[ss:] with - contribution. So:
```
if s>=0:
  G[:n-s, m] += 1 - h[s:]

Pair CD implementation details:
```
def pair_cd(h, n, passes, deadline, evals=24, pivot_mode='best', seed=42):
    h=h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h)
    best_c=c.max(); lags = np.arange(n-1, -n, -1) # length M: n-1,...,-(n-1)? Wait m=0 s=n-1; m=M-1 s=-(n-1). Use s_array = (n-1) - np.arange(M). 
```
For pair gradient a for p,r:
Using s = s_array (where c[m]=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+s})). Derivative wrt variable x: (1-h[x+s]) - h[x-s]. So:
`a = np.empty(M); a.fill(0); q=p+s; valid=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[valid]+=1-h[q[valid]]; q=p-s; valid=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[valid]-=h[q[valid]];`
For r subtract. To avoid allocating four q? We can compute for p and r separately calls `_slope_vector(h, x, s)`.
```
def slope_vector(h,x,s):
  n=len(h); a=np.zeros_like(s,float)
  q=x+s; v=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[v]+=1-h[q[v]]
  q=x-s; v=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[v]-=h[q[v]]
  return a
```
This allocates q arrays. `s` is int array. Could precompute `s_pos`, `s_neg`? But fine.
Then `a = slope_vector(h,p,s) - slope_vector(h,r,s)`.
M up to 1399; 2 calls per p. Each call 2 bool q and 2 index adds. 4 operations per call ->8*1399 per p ~10k element ops; 700=7M per pass. Plus line search 24*M=24M, dominating.

Line search function to avoid function call overhead within eval? We'll implement inside loop:
```
def fval(d, base, a, q0, q0lin, pm1, ama1, qa1? ):

`pair_cd` signature:
```
def pair_cd(h, n, deadline, max_passes, eval_grid=15, eval_golden=14, rng=None, use_avg_pivot=True):

Inside, `dt = 2.0/n`; `best_c = compute raw* dt`. But to avoid scaling in loops, keep `best_raw`. Start c array.
Pseudo:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,max_passes,grid_pts=15,gold_iters=14,rng=None):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    target = n/2.0
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    best_raw = float(c.max())
    dx=2.0/n
    s = (n-1) - np.arange(2*n-1, dtype=float) maybe int? Use int for index. For q = x+s, s int array.
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    for pass_i in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       # pivot choose
       if pass_i % 7 == 6:
           pivot = int(rng.randint(n))
       else:
           slack = np.minimum(h,1-h)
           pivot = int(np.argmax(slack))
           if slack[pivot] < 1e-10:
              pivot = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)))
       perm = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)[::pass_i%2+1?]
       improved = False
       for p in perm:
           if p == pivot: continue
           hp = h[p]; hr = h[pivot]
           dlo = max(-hp, hr-1.0)
           dhi = min(1.0-hp, hr)
           if dhi - dlo <= 1e-12: continue
           dlo += 1e-14; dhi -= 1e-14
           if dhi <= dlo: continue
           # slopes
           a = slope_diff(h,p,pivot,s)
           M=2*n-1; diag=n-1
           m1 = diag + p - pivot
           m2 = diag + pivot - p
           if not (0<=m1<M and 0<=m2<M): continue # should
           if m1 == m2: m2=-1
           base = c
           # Need base diag set -inf without modifying c. We can copy base = c.copy(); base[diag]=-inf
           # But copying M per p cost. Could use np.where? `base = c; base = base.copy()` yes.
           base = c.copy(); base[diag] = -1e100
           q0c = float(c[diag]); q0l = float(a[diag])
           c1=float(c[m1]); a1=float(a[m1]); c2=float(c[m2]); a2=a[m2] if valid else -inf? if m2 invalid set None
           # current f0:
           f0 = max(float(np.max(base)), q0c, c1, c2)
           # minimize
           d,bf = _pair_minimize(dlo,dhi,base,a,q0c,q0l,c1,a1,c2,a2,grid_pts,gold_iters)
           if bf < f0 - 1e-12:
               c += d*a
               c[m1] += d*d
               if m2>=0: c[m2] += d*d
               c[diag] -= 2*d*d
               h[p] += d
               h[pivot] -= d
               improved=True
               # optional recompute c if drift? no
       # after pass
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
       # fix sum
       diff = target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
           h = project_feasible(h,n)
       c = np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax < best_raw:
           best_h = h.copy(); best_raw=cmax
       if not improved:
           break
    return best_h, best_raw*dx
```
Need `slope_diff` top-level:
```
def slope_diff(h,x,y,s):
    n=len(h); M=s.size; a=np.zeros(M)
    q=x+s; v=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[v]+=1-h[q[v]]
    q=x-s; v=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[v]-=h[q[v]]
    q=y+s; v=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[v]-=1-h[q[v]]
    q=y-s; v=(q>=0)&(q<n); a[v]+=h[q[v]]
    return a
```
Because pair change is h_x +d, h_y -d: derivative = g_x - g_y. g_y = (1-h[y+s]) - h[y-s]; subtract => - (1-h[y+s]) + h[y-s] = -1+h[y+s]+h[y-s]. In code above subtract first and add second correct.
But q arrays with s int; `x+s` array int. Good.
Potential performance: four boolean arrays and indexing per p. Could combine q_x_s and q_y_s? Not necessary.

def _pair_eval(d, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1,a1,c2,a2):
    v = np.max(base + d*a)
    t = q0c + q0l*d - 2*d*d
    if t > v: v=t
    t = c1 + a1*d + d*d
    if t > v: v=t
    if a2 is not None? Use -1e100 if invalid.
```

def _pair_minimize(dlo,dhi,base,a,q0c,q0l,c1,a1,c2,a2,grid_pts,gold_iters):
    best_d=0.0; best_f=_pair_eval(0.0,...)
    # grid
    if dhi-dlo > 1e-12:
       ds = np.linspace(dlo,dhi,grid_pts)
       best_i=0
       for i,d in enumerate(ds):
          f=_pair_eval(d,...)
          if f<best_f: best_f=f; best_d=d; best_i=i
       # bracket
       lo = ds[max(0,best_i-1)]; hi=ds[min(grid_pts-1,best_i+1)]
       # golden
       gr=(math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
       if hi-lo > 1e-14:
          d1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); d2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
          f1=_pair_eval(d1...); f2=...
          for _ in range(gold_iters):
             if f1 < f2:
                hi=d2; d2=d1; f2=f1; d1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f1=...
             else:
                lo=d1; d1=d2; f1=f2; d2=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f2=...
             dm=(lo+hi)*0.5; fm=...; update best
    return best_d,best_f

Now LP gradient matrix: Need use correct s. We'll implement:
```
def gradient_matrix(h,n):
 M=2*n-1; G=np.empty((n,M),float)
 for m in range(M):
   s = n-1-m
   col=m
   if s>=0:
      G[:,col]=0
      if n-s>0: G[:n-s,col] += 1-h[s:]
      if n-s>0: G[s:,col] -= h[:n-s]
   else:
      ss=-s
      G[:,col]=0
      G[ss:,col] += 1-h[:n-ss]
      G[:n-ss,col] -= h[ss:]
 return G
```
But using `G[:,col]=0` inside loop and then slice += requires G initialized zeros. If `G=np.zeros((n,M))`, no need zero. Loop:
```
if s>=0:
  if s<n: G[:n-s, col] += 1-h[s:]; G[s:, col] -= h[:n-s]
else: ...
```
Since zeros initially. For s=0, both full slices: G[:n]+=1-h; G[:n]-=h =>1-2h. Good.
Performance: 1399 loops, each two slice additions length n-s. For n=700, ~500k additions, okay. But Python loop 1399 manageable. Could precompute slices lists for n to avoid conditions. For each n, build list of (s, rowstart1, rowend1, hstart1, hend1, rowstart2,...). But n varies. Simpler:

`lp_step`:
```
def lp_step(h,n,time_left):
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,0)
    M = len(c)
    G = gradient_matrix(h,n)
    rows = np.arange(M)
    A = np.empty((M,n+1)); A[:,:n]=G; A[:,n]=-1
    b = G.dot(h) - c
    c_obj = np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
    A_eq = np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,n]=0
    bounds = [(0,1)]*n + [(None,None)]
    tl = max(1.0, min(20.0, time_left/3.0))
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=[n/2.0],bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':tl})
    except: try simplex...
    if res is not None and res.x is not None:
       u=res.x[:n]
       u=project_feasible(u,n)
       return u
 return h
```
Maybe linprog with all constraints and dense A; time limit could abort with best? HiGHS may return status 3 (time limit) with x maybe open solution? We can use if `res.x` not None. If status not 0 but x feasible? HiGHS may have primal solution. We can check `res.status not in (3? )`? Use if `res.x is not None`.

def _pair_eval(d, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2):
    v = float(np.max(base + d*a))
    t = q0c + q0l*d - 2.0*d*d
    if t > v: v = t
    if c1 > -1e90:
        t = c1 + a1*d + d*d
        if t > v: v = t
    if c2 > -1e90:
        t = c2 + a2*d + d*d
        if t > v: v = t
    return v
```

def _pair_minimize(dlo,dhi,base,a,q0c,q0l,c1,a1,c2,a2,grid_pts=15,gold_iters=14):

    best_f=_pair_eval(0.0, ...)

        ds=np.linspace(dlo,dhi,grid_pts)
        bi=0

        if bi>0: lo=float(ds[bi-1])
        else: lo=dlo

G column for variable p full lag m derivative at h: g_p = (1-h[p+s]) - h[p-s] where s=n-1-m. Pair CD slope a = g_p - g_r. LP constraints signs: c[m]=∑h_i(1-h_{i+s}); linearization c(u)≈c + G_col·? Actually G matrix rows variables p, columns m: G[p,m]=∂c_m/∂h_p. Then c_m(u)≈c_m + G[:,m]·(u-h). Constraint ≤ t: G·u - t ≤ G·h - c. Correct.

LP with dense A: `A_ub = G.T?` Wait G shape (n,M). We need for each m row length n: G[:,m]. So A_ub = G.T with shape (M,n). Then append -1. In code, `A[:, :n] = G.T` not G. Important! Earlier I wrote G rows but G is n x M. So `A = np.empty((M,n+1)); A[:, :n] = G.T`; b = G.T @ h - c = (G.dot(h)? G n xM dot h n gives n? No, `G.T @ h` length M, or `G.T.dot(h)`. In Python, `G.T @ h`. Or `np.einsum('im,i->m', G, h)`. Use `Gh = G.T @ h`.
Alternatively define G as (M,n) directly to simplify. Let's define `gradient_matrix` returns G shape (M,n): each row m length n. Then `A_ub[:, :n] = G`, `b = G.dot(h) - c`. Easier.
Implement:
```
def gradient_matrix(h,n):
 M=2*n-1; G=np.zeros((M,n))
 for m in range(M):
   s=n-1-m
   if s>=0:
      G[m, :n-s] += 1 - h[s:]
      G[m, s:] -= h[:n-s]
   else:
      ss=-s
      G[m, ss:] += 1 - h[:n-ss]
      G[m, :n-ss] -= h[ss:]
 return G
```
Check s>=0: variable p: q1=p+s valid p<n-s => column p; q2=p-s valid p>=s. Good. s<0: q1=p-ss valid p>=ss; q2=p+ss valid p<n-ss. Good. Row m uses +=/ -=; initialized zeros. For s=0 full: row +=1-h; row -=h =>1-2h.
This loops M rows, each row vector operations length n. G shape M x n. Then A_ub = np.column_stack((G, -np.ones(M))). b=G.dot(h)-c. Good.

LP memory: G 1399x700 ~1MB; A ~1MB.

Time for gradient loop: 1399 iterations with vector length up to700; each iteration two slice ops. In Python, 1399 iterations okay (~0.005s? maybe 0.05s). Fine.

`project_feasible` bisection:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12:
        return h
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # We want sum(clip(h - tau)) = target. sum decreases with tau.
    # Check bounds:
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(h-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # if still not due to extremes, fallback iterative
    diff=target-h.sum()
    for _ in range(30):
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h+diff*room/tot,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h+diff*h/tot,0,1)
    return h
```
The bisection h-mid with mid in [-1,1]: If h all small and target high, need mid negative to increase. lo=-1 gives h+1 clipped max n maybe >target; hi=1 gives h-1 clipped min 0 < target. Good. If h includes values >1? clipped. 60 iterations over n small. Could be called many times, 60*700=42k per call; LP line search 5 calls, okay. Pair_cd calls project once per pass; 20 passes 840k, fine.

`c5_raw`:
```
def c5_raw(h,n): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')))
def c5_value(h,n): return c5_raw(h,n)*2.0/n
```

`lp_step`: Need maybe active rows. Use all.
```
def lp_step(h,n,time_left):
    M=2*n-1
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    G=gradient_matrix(h,n)
    # optional rows = np.argsort(c)[-max(200,M//2):]
    rows=np.arange(M)
    if len(rows)>800: # maybe central? Actually keep all? For speed, central top 800 by c? Could harm. Use all for n<=700.
    A=np.empty((len(rows), n+1))
    A[:,:n]=G[rows]
    A[:,n]=-1.0
    b=G[rows].dot(h) - c[rows]
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
    A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,n]=0.0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n + [(None,None)]
    tl=max(1.0,min(30.0, time_left/4.0))
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=[n/2.0],bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':tl})
    except Exception:
       try: res=linprog(...,method='revised simplex')
       except: return None
    x=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else None
    if x is None: return None
    u=project_feasible(x[:n],n)
    return u
```
If active rows subset, objective may ignore important constraints, candidate may violate. But line search actual may reject if worse. Could still help. Maybe for speed select rows with c >= Mraw - 0.03*n? But if choose subset, linearized min can push into region where ignored constraints become active. Since line search rejects actual worsening, safe. We can use all initially; for n=700 all okay. Skip active.

`structured_h`:
```
def structured_h(n, kind, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    if kind==0:
       h=1.0 - np.abs(x-1.0)
    elif kind==1:
       h=0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x)
    elif kind==2:
       h=0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)
    elif kind==3:
       h=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(np.pi*x)
    else:
       h=0.5 + rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Kind 3: sin πx zero edges center 0.85; area 1? project. Fine.

`lp_polish` helper:
```
def lp_polish(h,n,deadline,rng=None):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_raw=c5_raw(h,n); dx=2/n; fail=0
   while time.time() < deadline:
      rem=deadline-time.time()
      if rem < 30: break
      u=lp_step(h,n, rem)
      if u is None:
         break
      if np.max(np.abs(u-h)) < 1e-13:
         break
      cand_best=h; cand_best_raw=best_raw
      for alpha in (1.0,0.66,0.44,0.25,0.125):
         cand=(1-alpha)*h + alpha*u
         cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
         raw=c5_raw(cand,n)
         if raw < cand_best_raw:
            cand_best=cand; cand_best_raw=raw
      if cand_best_raw < best_raw - 1e-12:
         h=cand_best; best_raw=cand_best_raw; fail=0
      else:
         fail += 1
         h=project_feasible(u,n) # move for exploration? maybe only up to 3
         if fail >= 4: break
   return h, best_raw*2.0/n
```
If we set h=u even when no improve, we may drift; but maintain best_raw. The returned h should be best, not working h if working worse. We currently return h (possibly worse). Need return best_h separately.

...
return best_h,best_raw*dx
```

```
def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,max_passes,rng=None,grid_pts=21,gold_iters=16):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    # ensure nearly feasible? Don't project? Use h as is. If h infeasible from previous LP maybe project before call. We'll project at start? It may change objective; okay. Let's project start to feasible.
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(...); best_raw=...
    dx=2/n; best_h=h.copy()
    s=(n-1)-np.arange(2*n-1,dtype=int)
    for pass...
       ...
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
       # fix sum by tiny adjustment? maybe not.
       # But if sum drift >1e-10, apply project? Could harm. We can adjust one variable? Simpler if abs(target-h.sum())>1e-9: h=project_feasible(h,n)
       c_re = np.correlate(h,1-h)
       cmax = float(c_re.max())
       if cmax < best_raw - 1e-12: update best_h=h.copy(); c=c_re; best_raw=cmax
       else:
           h=best_h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h); best_raw=c5_raw
       if not improved: break
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    raw=c5_raw(best_h,n)
    return best_h, raw*2.0/n
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(h.sum() - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    for _ in range(35):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def c5_raw(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))

def c5_value(h, n):
    return c5_raw(h, n) * (2.0 / n)

def gradient_matrix(h, n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    G = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=float)
    base = n - 1
    for idx in range(m):
        s = base - idx
        if s >= 0:
            row = G[idx]
            row[:n - s] += 1.0 - h[s:]
            row[s:] -= h[:n - s]
        else:
            ss = -s
            row = G[idx]
            row[ss:] += 1.0 - h[:n - ss]
            row[:n - ss] -= h[ss:]
    return G

def lp_step(h, n, time_left):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    G = gradient_matrix(h, n)
    rows = np.arange(c.size)
    A = np.empty((c.size, n + 1), dtype=float)
    A[:, :n] = G[rows]
    A[:, n] = -1.0
    b = G[rows].dot(h) - c[rows]
    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0
    A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
    A_eq[0, n] = 0.0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(None, None)]
    tl = max(1.0, min(25.0, time_left / 4.0))
    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[0.5 * n],
                      bounds=bounds, method="highs", options={"time_limit": tl})
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None
    u = res.x[:n]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return None
    return project_feasible(u, n)

def lp_polish(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_raw = c5_raw(h, n)
    fail = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        u = lp_step(h, n, rem)
        if u is None:
            break
        if np.max(np.abs(u - h)) < 1e-13:
            break
        cand_h = h
        cand_raw = best_raw
        for alpha in (1.0, 0.55, 0.35, 0.18, 0.08):
            cand = (1.0 - alpha) * h + alpha * u
            cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
            r = c5_raw(cand, n)
            if r < cand_raw:
                cand_h = cand
                cand_raw = r
        if cand_raw < best_raw - 1e-12:
            best_h = cand_h
            best_raw = cand_raw
            h = best_h.copy()
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail >= 3:
                break
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return best_h, best_raw * dx

def _pair_eval(d, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2):
    v = float(np.max(base + d * a))
    t = q0c + q0l * d - 2.0 * d * d
    if t > v:
        v = t
    t = c1 + a1 * d + d * d
    if t > v:
        v = t
    t = c2 + a2 * d + d * d
    if t > v:
        v = t
    return v

def _pair_minimize(dlo, dhi, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2,
                   grid_pts, gold_iters):
    best_d = 0.0
    best_f = _pair_eval(0.0, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
    if dhi - dlo > 1e-14:
        ds = np.linspace(dlo, dhi, grid_pts)
        bi = 0
        for i in range(grid_pts):
            d = float(ds[i])
            f = _pair_eval(d, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
            if f < best_f:
                best_f = f
                best_d = d
                bi = i
        left = max(0, bi - 2)
        right = min(grid_pts - 1, bi + 2)
        lo = float(ds[left])
        hi = float(ds[right])
        if hi - lo > 1e-14:
            gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
            d1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            d2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            f1 = _pair_eval(d1, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
            f2 = _pair_eval(d2, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
            for _ in range(gold_iters):
                if hi - lo < 1e-15:
                    break
                if f1 < f2:
                    hi = d2
                    d2 = d1
                    f2 = f1
                    d1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
                    f1 = _pair_eval(d1, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
                else:
                    lo = d1
                    d1 = d2
                    f1 = f2
                    d2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
                    f2 = _pair_eval(d2, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
                dm = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                fm = _pair_eval(dm, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2)
                if fm < best_f:
                    best_f = fm
                    best_d = dm
    return best_d, best_f

def slope_diff(h, x, y, s):
    n = len(h)
    a = np.zeros(s.size, dtype=float)
    q = x + s
    v = (q >= 0) & (q < n)
    a[v] += 1.0 - h[q[v]]
    q = x - s
    v = (q >= 0) & (q < n)
    a[v] -= h[q[v]]
    q = y + s
    v = (q >= 0) & (q < n)
    a[v] -= 1.0 - h[q[v]]
    q = y - s
    v = (q >= 0) & (q < n)
    a[v] += h[q[v]]
    return a

def pair_cd(h, n, deadline, max_passes, rng=None, grid_pts=21, gold_iters=16):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    best_raw = float(np.max(c))
    best_h = h.copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    m = 2 * n - 1
    diag = n - 1
    s = (n - 1) - np.arange(m, dtype=int)
    NEG = -1.0e100
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(1234567)

    for pass_i in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        slack = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h)
        if slack.max() > 1.0e-8:
            pivot = int(np.argmax(slack))
        elif pass_i % 2 == 0:
            pivot = int(np.argmin(h))
        else:
            pivot = int(np.argmax(h))

        perm = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False
        for idx in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            p = int(perm[idx])
            if p == pivot:
                continue
            hp = float(h[p])
            hr = float(h[pivot])
            dlo = max(-hp, hr - 1.0)
            dhi = min(1.0 - hp, hr)
            if dhi - dlo <= 1.0e-13:
                continue
            dlo += 1.0e-14
            dhi -= 1.0e-14
            if dhi <= dlo:
                continue

            a = slope_diff(h, p, pivot, s)
            m1 = diag + p - pivot
            m2 = diag + pivot - p
            if not (0 <= m1 < m and 0 <= m2 < m):
                continue

            base = c.copy()
            base[diag] = NEG
            q0c = float(c[diag])
            q0l = float(a[diag])
            c1 = float(c[m1])
            a1 = float(a[m1])
            c2 = float(c[m2])
            a2 = float(a[m2])

            f0 = float(np.max(c))
            d, bf = _pair_minimize(dlo, dhi, base, a, q0c, q0l, c1, a1, c2, a2,
                                   grid_pts, gold_iters)
            if bf < f0 - 1.0e-12:
                c += d * a
                c[m1] += d * d
                c[m2] += d * d
                c[diag] -= 2.0 * d * d
                h[p] += d
                h[pivot] -= d
                improved = True

        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(h.sum() - 0.5 * n) > 1.0e-9:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax < best_raw - 1.0e-12:
            best_raw = cmax
            best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        if not improved:
            break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_raw = c5_raw(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_raw * dx

def structured_h(n, kind, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    if kind == 0:
        h = 1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0)
    elif kind == 1:
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 2:
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    else:
        ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        f1 = 1.0 + 0.5 * rng.random()
        f2 = 2.0 + 1.0 * rng.random()
        h = 0.5 + 0.18 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + ph1) + 0.10 * np.sin(
            2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + ph2)
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    end = t0 + float(budget_s)
    deadline = end - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        h = structured_h(n, 0, rng)

    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, n)
    best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    main_end = deadline - 185.0
    if main_end < t0 + 240.0:
        main_end = deadline - 35.0

    if time.time() < main_end - 260.0:
        lp_dead = min(main_end - 240.0, time.time() + 95.0)
        hh, cc = lp_polish(best_h, n, lp_dead)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc
            best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    if time.time() < main_end - 140.0:
        hh, cc = pair_cd(best_h, n, min(main_end - 140.0, time.time() + 520.0),
                         70, rng, 21, 16)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc
            best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    if time.time() < main_end - 90.0:
        lp_dead = min(main_end - 75.0, time.time() + 75.0)
        hh, cc = lp_polish(best_h, n, lp_dead)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc
            best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    if time.time() < main_end:
        hh, cc = pair_cd(best_h, n, main_end, 90, rng, 23, 17)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc
            best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    alt_end = deadline - 25.0
    try_n = [n - 100, n + 100, 450, 550, 650, 800, 350]
    seen = set()
    ordered = []
    for nn in sorted(set(try_n), key=lambda z: -abs(z - len(best_h))):
        if 80 <= nn <= 950 and nn not in seen:
            seen.add(nn)
            ordered.append(nn)
    for nn in ordered:
        if time.time() > alt_end - 80.0:
            break
        rem = alt_end - time.time() - 30.0
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        secs = min(75.0, rem)
        if nn > 750:
            secs = min(secs, 50.0)
        sub_dead = time.time() + secs
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nn)
        h_try = np.interp(x_new, best_x, best_h)
        h_try = project_feasible(h_try, nn)
        hp, cp = pair_cd(h_try, nn, sub_dead, 26, rng, 17, 12)
        if cp < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hp, cp
            best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    if time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
        for kind in (0, 1, 2):
            if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
                break
            nn = min(700, max(350, len(best_h)))
            sub_dead = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 35.0)
            hs = structured_h(nn, kind, rng)
            hp, cp = pair_cd(hs, nn, sub_dead, 18, rng, 15, 11)
            if cp < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hp, cp
                best_x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
